HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B007","root_001543/B001","root_001643/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3bf12ff680b39430d446","text":"3. **Exploratory: attached liability falling away before public repair.** Fixed `ن ق ض/B001` is undoing what had been made firm, and fixed `ظ ه ر/B013` is the idiom of blame or defect ceasing to adhere to a person. The preceding **عنك وزرك** supplies separation from a burden that can include sin under `و ز ر/B002`; 94:4 then raises `ذ ك ر/B007`, honorable repute. The fixed branches supply undoing and non-adhering blame, while the adjacent ayat supply removal and restored standing. **Abductive move:** I infer moral liability -> detached blame -> repaired reputation; this is a form-distant activation because the fixed surface is the noun **ظهرك**, not the packet's idiom. **Reading change:** the broken-back image acquires a social-moral layer in which what clung to the person is undone before mention is elevated.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000970/B002","root_001543/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_60191e637f75e1e657a2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:3:2\",\"root_001543\",[\"B005\"]],[\"94:3:3\",\"root_000970\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Belin çıkardığı ses, görünmeyen yükün bedeni çökme sınırına getirdiğini duyulur kılan bir aşırı yüklenme belirtisidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000970/B002","root_001543/B005","root_001643/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_77cf5b1bb55bdd7edba3","text":"1. **The back's sound as a diagnostic of hidden overload.** Fixed `ن ق ض/B005` is specifically the creaking or cracking of joints, saddles, loads, and a back under weight; fixed `ظ ه ر/B002` is the anatomical back. The antecedent in 94:2 is `و ز ر/B002`, a heavy burden, and 94:4-6 reverse pressure through raising and ease. The sound branch makes strain perceptible, while the back branch identifies the load-bearing surface. **Abductive move:** I infer that the audible symptom reveals a threshold of failure and motivates the unloading; causation is not grammatically asserted beyond the relative clause. **Reading change:** **أنقض** is not merely “undid” the back but makes overload audible, turning the ayah into a diagnosis.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000970/B005","root_001543/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7f230123882abe4c2ac9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:3:2\",\"root_001543\",[\"B002\"]],[\"94:3:3\",\"root_000970\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yolculuktan tükenmiş binek ve yük taşıyan sırt çağrışımları, ayeti uzun bir görevin yorduğu taşıma gücü olarak okutur; sonraki çaba ve yöneliş bu yolculuğun yeniden başlamasıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001543/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_82f422b2d9ca73f9aaef","text":"2. **A transport body worn down by its journey.** Fixed `ن ق ض/B002` is a camel exhausted by travel, and fixed `ظ ه ر/B005` is a mount or load-bearing animal kept for travel. Around them, 94:2 offers `و ض ع/B003` rapid mounted travel, 94:5-6 offer `ي س ر/B005` light compliant movement, and 94:7 offers `ف ر غ/B003` breadth of gait plus `ن ص ب/B010` a sustained march; 94:8 supplies a destination. The fixed branches cast the back as transport capacity and its damage as journey-wear. **Abductive move:** I infer that these animal-motion branches describe one continuous traveler whose load is removed so movement can resume. **Reading change:** the ayah can portray exhausted conveyance, not only an injured human back, and the surah's later imperatives become resumed travel.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000970/B013","root_001543/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_85a1e1dd21934244b6c3","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:3:2\",\"root_001543\",[\"B001\"]],[\"94:3:3\",\"root_000970\",[\"B013\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Çözülme ve kişiye yapışmış kusurun düşmesi çağrışımları, kaldırılan yükü sonraki itibar onarımından önce ayrılan bir suçlama veya sorumluluk gibi gösterebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B011","root_001543/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a1b21a786e6a0e743a80","text":"94:7 retrospectively made the travel-worn animal branch of `ن ق ض B002` more visible: after the back has creaked, the command does not cancel movement but reorders it. 94:4 also surprised the reading of `ظهر`: the strained support is answered not merely by relief but by public elevation of `ذكر`, so hidden pressure becomes manifest honor. 94:8 strengthened the covenant/support reading by making `ر ب ب B011` available as a repaired binding relation after the old backing had nearly come undone.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000970/B001","root_000970/B008","root_001543/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b1c43dc9b02d80cf7c95","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:3:2\",\"root_001543\",[\"B005\"]],[\"94:3:3\",\"root_000970\",[\"B001\",\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Gıcırdayarak dışa vuran bel, içeride saklı kalan baskıyı görünür ve bilinir hâle getirir; bedensel ses aynı zamanda gizli sıkıntının açığa çıkışıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000970/B006","root_000970/B016","root_001543/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c91158143ee55161dd4c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:3:2\",\"root_001543\",[\"B001\"]],[\"94:3:3\",\"root_000970\",[\"B006\",\"B016\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Ağırlık, yalnız bedeni değil, kişiyi ayakta tutan toplumsal veya bağlılığa dayalı destek düzenini de çözülme noktasına getiren bir baskı kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B011","root_000970/B001","root_000970/B002","root_000970/B005","root_000970/B006","root_001507/B010","root_001543/B001","root_001543/B002","root_001543/B005","root_001643/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_db95dc99ad2295973e92","text":"1. The direct image is a back audibly strained by a load. Lexical evidence: `ن ق ض B005` creaking under strain from joints, loads, saddles, or a burdened back; `ظ ه ر B002` anatomical back; 94:2 `و ز ر B002` heavy burden. Structural cue: the relative phrase in 94:3 specifies the `وزر` of 94:2 by its effect. Assigned role: `أنقض` supplies audible evidence of pressure; `ظهر` supplies the support surface. Abductive move supplied by me: sound is treated as the symptom of hidden load. Reading change: the ayah makes prior suffering materially audible, not merely conceptually heavy. 2. The burden also threatens the collapse of a constructed order. Lexical evidence: `ن ق ض B001` breaking or undoing what was made firm, such as rope, structure, covenant, oath, argument; `ظ ه ر B006/B016` backing/support and supporting people; `ر ب ب B011` covenant or pact as binding bundle. Structural cue: the passage later raises mention (94:4) and directs desire to the Lord (94:8), after the old support has been strained. Assigned role: `نقض` is undoing; `ظهر` is the system of backing. Abductive move supplied by me: a burden can break not only a body but the social/covenantal support on which a person stands. Reading change: the ayah reads as near-unraveling of support, not only back pain. 3. Travel-weariness enters through animal and road branches. Lexical evidence: `ن ق ض B002` a camel worn down by travel; `ظ ه ر B005` mounts and spare riding animals; `و ض ع B003` urging a mount into rapid travel; `ن ص ب B010` day-long easy march; `ف ر غ B003` breadth in a road. Structural cue: later 94:7 gives a post-relief imperative that can be read as renewed movement/labor. Assigned role: the back is also a mount/backing that has been overtraveled. Abductive move supplied by me: the strained back is a vehicle image for mission-fatigue, preparing for a different kind of onward travel. Reading change: the ayah becomes exhaustion from carrying a journey, not only static weight. 4. A disclosure reading is activated by `ظهر`. Lexical evidence: `ظ ه ر B001` coming out into view, `ظ ه ر B008` finding out/access to hidden matter, and `ش ر ح B001` opening what was hidden. Structural cue: 94:1 opens the chest; 94:3 names the back/surface; 94:4 raises mention into public view. Assigned role: the pressure on the back brings an invisible burden into manifestation. Abductive move supplied by me: what creaks becomes known; the body exposes what was hidden. Reading change: the ayah reads as revelation of hidden strain as well as the strain itself.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B007","root_001543/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ebd15960aa4e758937e1","text":"94:4 made the social extension newly visible: raised `ذ ك ر/B007` can follow the falling-away of burden or blame, so bodily repair and public repair may run together.
